# 06 — API implementation plan (controlled commerce API)

**Purpose.** Defines the business API contracts between the storefront (`P-S*`), ERP staff workspace (`P-E*`),
vendor portal (`P-V*`), external providers (webhooks) and the operational core. Future sessions implement endpoints
from this file by ID (`API-M##-##`); backend behaviour behind each endpoint is in `05-backend.md` (business rules
`BR-M##-##`), data in `03-database.md`, permissions in `07-auth-roles-permissions.md`.

**Sources used.** BP §6.3–6.5, §7.3–7.5, §8, §9, §10, §11, §12, §13, §14, §16.3–16.5, §17.4–17.6, §18, §19.1, §21,
§23.1 · PR1 §6–§11, §14 · PR2 §3–§9 · MEET · mockup pages `store-*`, `erp-*`, `vendor-*` and `assets/tradex.js`
(shells). **Status & evidence labels:** `00-conventions.md` §2–§3. **Decisions:** `DECISIONS.md` (D-001…D-209, D-220…D-222);
this file proposed D-141…D-154 (§6; D-150 in `05-backend.md` §7). **Permission keys:** `07-auth-roles-permissions.md`
(the source for keys, role cells and guards). **Gap resolution** of the second-wave plan files: §8.

**Global rule.** BP §17.4: *"Exact routes depend on the chosen framework. These are business contracts, not direct
ERP CRUD permissions."* Every path below is **provisional** (`/v1/...` pattern from BP §17.4) until **D-080** is
decided; the business contract (inputs, guards, outputs, errors) is what must be implemented.

---

## 1. Conventions

### 1.1 API style
| # | Rule | Source |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Endpoints are **business operations** (quote, place order, receive goods, decide approval), never generic ERP document CRUD. Do not expose arbitrary ERP document writes to the storefront, vendor portal or chat. | BP §17.4, §15.4 ("Explicit narrow endpoints in the extension app") |
| 2 | One API serves all channels (web, staff, WhatsApp, future app); pricing, stock and order rules are server-side and shared. | BP §8.1, §16.1, §28.4 (Phase 2 app reuses Phase 1 APIs) |
| 3 | Where a P-E screen is implemented with **native ERP screens** (D-004), the staff endpoints listed for that screen may be replaced by native ERP operations, but every business guard in this file and `05-backend.md` still applies (BP §16.3 "do not update core tables directly to bypass validations"). | BP §30.1, D-004 |
| 4 | The same resource may serve several audiences (e.g. `GET /v1/orders/{id}` for customer, dealer member and staff); the **caller's authorised context** decides what is returned. | BP §17.4 ("Ownership/organisation/role checks") |
| 5 | Internal module calls (e.g. M10 → M06 reservation) are in-process service calls inside the operational core, not HTTP; only the endpoints below are external contracts. | BP §15.5, §15.6, §16.1 |
| 6 | Path families (provisional, D-080): public & customer `/v1/...` and `/v1/me/...`; vendor portal `/v1/vendor/...` (BP §17.4 example); staff operations `/v1/...` on business resources (BP §17.4 `/v1/receipts`, `/v1/approvals/{id}/decision`); provider callbacks `/v1/webhooks/<provider-type>` (BP §17.4). | BP §17.4 |

### 1.2 Authentication (D-083 session/token mechanism; D-040 methods — both OPEN)
Auth class codes used in the catalogue:

| Code | Caller | Mechanism (logical) | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PUB` | Anyone (guest) | No credential; rate-limited (D-084) | BP §3.1 Guest, §19.1 |
| `CUS` | Consumer or dealer member (customer account) | Authenticated customer session (D-083); dealer context only via verified business-account membership | BP §6.5, §8.3, §19.1 |
| `GAT` | Guest holding an **order-access token** | Token issued by API-M10-11 after order number + phone + OTP, or secure link (D-021) | BP §6.5, §13.4 |
| `LNK` | Holder of a secure checkout link | Single-purpose signed link token bound to a draft order (API-M10-20/22) | BP §13.2, §29.5 |
| `STF` | Staff user | Staff session; **MFA mandatory for privileged accounts** (R-owner, R-ops_admin, R-finance and any role granting permission edits) | BP §18.2, §20.1 |
| `VEN` | Vendor user (R-vendor_applicant / R-supplier) | Vendor-portal session; always scoped to one vendor organisation | BP §3.1, §11.1–11.2 |
| `INT` | Machine client (R-integration: supplier feed / vendor API key, legacy POS) | Narrow credential per integration; no interactive admin | BP §3.1 Integration account, §16.4 |
| `SIG` | Payment / shipping / WhatsApp / messaging provider | Raw-body signature or provider authentication per contract | BP §10.2, §16.4, §17.4 |

Rules: cookie-authenticated mutations need CSRF protection; CORS is configured deliberately (BP §19.1). Private
responses are never cached under shared keys (BP §8.4, §16.5). Rate limits on sign-in, OTP, order-access and
checkout (BP §19.1; values D-084). Never log passwords, tokens, payment data or full chat content (BP §19.1).

### 1.3 Authorisation
- Enforced **on the server for every business operation**, at object level (can this caller touch this record?)
  and property level (may this caller set this field?) (BP §19.1, OWASP S14). Out-of-scope objects return
  `404 NOT_FOUND` so that existence is not disclosed (BP §23.1 T11 "Access denied without exposing data").
- Roles are the IDs of `00-conventions.md` §9 (`R-*`). Record scope qualifiers used in the catalogue:
  `own` (caller's own records), `org` (caller's business account or vendor organisation), `loc` (caller's assigned
  locations; cross-branch visibility per D-029), `assigned` (tasks/queues assigned to the caller), `all`.
- Monetary/approval limits come from E-approval_threshold (client-supplied, **D-024**); the API returns
  `202 APPROVAL_REQUIRED` with an approval request instead of executing when the caller's authority is exceeded
  (BP §12.5 flow, §18.1).
- Separation of duties: the initiator of a high-risk request cannot approve it where feasible (BP §18.2).
- Detailed permission keys per role: `07-auth-roles-permissions.md` (authority matrix BP §18.1).

### 1.4 Server-derived context — fields the API never trusts (BP §17.4, §8.4, §17.5)
| Client-sent field (ignored / rejected) | Server source of truth |
|---|---|
| `is_admin`, role, permission flags | Session principal → E-user_role_assignment (M02) |
| `dealer_approved`, customer type/segment, price list | Session → E-business_account_member → E-business_account (status approved) → assigned E-price_list (M08/M05); T10 |
| `unit_price`, discount, tax, shipping, totals | M05 PricingEngine (quote version) at quote and again at order/reservation (BP §8.1 steps 1–8) |
| `stock_quantity`, availability, ATP | M06 stock authority (reservation at order) (BP §9.1) |
| `seller_id`, offer owner | E-offer record (approved offer) (BP §17.1) |
| `refund_approved`, refund amount limit | M11 refund state machine + authority (BP §18.1, §17.6 refundable-amount invariant) |
| `channel` | Validated against the authenticated entry point (BP §17.5) |
| address ownership, business membership | Checked against session (BP §17.5) |

Requests carrying these fields are processed **as if the field were absent**; where the field would change the
outcome (e.g. a supplied price) the server recomputes and the response shows the authoritative value.

### 1.5 Idempotency (BP §10.2–10.3, §17.5; transport D-079)
- **Required** (`Idem = yes` in the catalogue) on every operation with money, stock or external side-effects:
  place order, payment attempt, cancellation, refund transitions/retries, goods receipt posting, transfer
  ship/receive, stock adjustment request, dispatch/handover, courier booking, return receipt, accounting-export
  retry, import commit/retry, vendor availability update, checkout-link send.
- Key transport (header name/contract) is **D-079** (OPEN). Key scope = caller + operation. The client generates
  one key per user intent and **reuses it on network retries** ("do not use a random new key on every network
  retry", BP §17.5).
- Same key + same payload → return the stored result (T06). Same key + different payload → `409
  IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`, the earlier effect is unchanged (BP §10.3). Stored in E-idempotency_record. Retention
  period: part of D-079.
- Server-to-provider calls also carry idempotency references (refund key, booking reference) (BP §10.3, T18, T20).

### 1.6 Optimistic concurrency
Mutations of versioned records (drafts, price lists, approvals, configuration) carry `expected_version`; mismatch →
`409 VERSION_CONFLICT` (BP §17.3 "optimistic concurrency version where useful"; BP §17.4 approval guard "expected
version"). Transport (body field vs header) → D-080.

### 1.7 Error model (envelope and HTTP mapping provisional — D-080)
Every error returns a stable **error code**, human message, `correlation_id` and optional `details` (field errors,
affected lines). `std` in the catalogue means: `400/422 VALIDATION_FAILED` · `401 UNAUTHENTICATED` · `403
FORBIDDEN` · `404 NOT_FOUND` (incl. out-of-scope) · `429 RATE_LIMITED`.

| Code | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `VALIDATION_FAILED` | Field/format/business validation failed; `details.fields[]` | BP §19.1 |
| `VERSION_CONFLICT` | `expected_version` stale | BP §17.3–17.4 |
| `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT` | Key reused with different payload | BP §10.3 |
| `STOCK_UNAVAILABLE` | Reservation failed for ≥1 line; `details.lines[{line, available}]` (accurate message) | BP §10.3, T04 |
| `PRICE_CHANGED` | Quote no longer valid; new quote returned for explicit acceptance | BP §6.3, §8.1 step 8 |
| `QUOTE_EXPIRED` / `RESERVATION_EXPIRED` | Time-limited quote/hold elapsed; re-check required | BP §8.1 step 7, §6.3 |
| `NOT_SERVICEABLE` | PIN/location cannot be served | BP §6.5 |
| `CONTEXT_INELIGIBLE` | Buyer context/location not eligible (e.g. dealer price after membership loss) — reprice with notice | BP §6.5, §8.4 |
| `POLICY_INELIGIBLE` | Return/cancellation outside policy | BP §6.3 Returns, §10.5 |
| `STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID` | Transition not allowed by the state machine guards | BP §10.1 |
| `APPROVAL_REQUIRED` (202) | Accepted as an approval request; not executed | BP §12.5, §18.1 |
| `AUTHORITY_EXCEEDED` (403) | Caller may not even request this | BP §18.1 |
| `STOCK_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE` (503) | Stock system unavailable → stop confirmation, never guess | BP §10.3 |
| `PROVIDER_PENDING` (202) | External provider outcome unknown; reconciliation scheduled | BP §10.3, §16.3 |
| `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` (503) | Provider down; fallback (manual) path available where documented | BP §16.4 |
| `SIGNATURE_INVALID` (401) | Webhook signature check failed; event not processed | BP §17.4 |
| `FEATURE_DISABLED` (404/409) | Conditional feature not enabled (decision/feature flag) | BP §20.5 feature flags; D-077 |
| `INVITATION_EXPIRED` / `CHALLENGE_EXPIRED` / `CODE_INVALID` | Token/OTP flows | MK store-login, erp-admin |

**BP §10.3 failure cases → API behaviour**

| Failure (BP §10.3) | Endpoint(s) | Required API behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|---|
| Two buyers compete for last unit | API-M10-06 | Exactly one reservation succeeds; other gets `409 STOCK_UNAVAILABLE` with accurate available qty | T04, T05 |
| Buyer double-clicks pay/order | API-M10-06, API-M11-01 | Same key → stored result returned | T06 |
| Same key with different basket | API-M10-06 | `409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`; earlier order untouched | T06 |
| Payment succeeds after reservation expiry | API-M11-03 → M10/M06 | Record payment; controlled re-reservation; else order `on_hold` + E-exception_case; resolve/refund per D-026 | T09 |
| Callback delivered twice | API-M11-03, API-M12-16 | `200` no-op (`duplicate`); one transition, one fulfilment task, no duplicate invoice | T07 |
| Failed event arrives after captured | API-M11-03 | Recorded as `ignored_out_of_order`; captured never downgraded | T08 |
| Worker crashes after sending request | outbox behind API-M11-10/-12, API-M12-11 | Reconcile by provider reference before retrying an irreversible action | T19, T20, T21 |
| Payment captured but app unavailable | API-M11-03 (provider retries), API-M11-06 | Durable provider events + reconciliation recover order state | T21 |
| Stock system unavailable | API-M10-06, confirmation path of API-M11-03 | `503 STOCK_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE` / hold confirmation; or segregated allocation (D-030) | T05 |
| Notification fails | M20 (API-M20-04 exposes) | Order stays valid; retry; delivery failure visible | — |
| Refund request times out | API-M11-10, API-M11-11, API-M11-12 | `202 PROVIDER_PENDING`; status query before any new refund | T18, T19 |
| Partial cancellation | API-M10-09 | Release only affected reservation; refund allocated amount once | T34 |

### 1.8 Webhooks (inbound provider events)
1. Read the **raw body**; verify signature/authentication **before** parsing or acting (BP §17.4 "Raw-body signature").
2. Persist the event with provider event id, type, received time, raw payload reference and signature result
   (E-payment_event / E-shipment_event / E-integration_event) — **deduplicate on provider event id** (BP §17.4,
   §10.3); a duplicate returns success with no effect.
3. Acknowledge quickly; apply the business effect idempotently in the same transaction as the event record or via
   E-outbox_operation (BP §16.3). Events can arrive out of order — apply only **legal** transitions; never
   downgrade a proven captured payment (BP §10.3, S09).
4. Preserve raw carrier/provider status and map to canonical states (BP §10.5).
5. Missing/late events are covered by scheduled reconciliation (BP §10.2 step 8; A09, A13).
Provider-specific signature schemes follow D-012 (payment), D-013 (shipping), D-014 (WhatsApp), D-015 (email/SMS).

### 1.9 Pagination, filtering, sorting (D-080)
`L` in the catalogue = standard list parameters: `page`/`page_size` **or** cursor (D-080), `sort`, `q` (text) plus
the named filters, and `include_counts?` (tab/facet/saved-view counts over the caller's scope — MK tab badges on
every workspace screen; definitions D-172/D-173; refresh D-146); response `{items[], page_info{…}, total?,
counts{}?}`. Filters are always intersected with the caller's
record scope. Staff queues ("saved views") are filter presets (API-M18-13/14). Exports are separate jobs with row
limits (API-M18-03; BP §14.4).

### 1.10 Money, dates, tax, identifiers
Money as integer minor units or fixed decimal — never floating point (BP §8.1); currency INR under D-059.
Tax-inclusive vs exclusive display per buyer type is **D-016**; responses carry both `tax_basis` and explicit tax
amounts. Timestamps with time zone; formats → D-080. External-facing numbers (order, RMA, PO) are display
references; internal IDs are immutable (BP §7.2 "immutable internal identifier").

### 1.11 Caching (BP §8.4, §16.5, T22)
Public catalog/content responses may be cached. Any response depending on buyer context (dealer price, quote,
cart, account, order, invoice) is `private, no-store` and never cached under a shared public URL.

### 1.12 Long-running work
Imports, exports, reconciliation, settlement matching, bulk actions and report schedules return `202` with a job
reference (E-import_job, E-export_job, E-job_attempt); status is read from the corresponding GET endpoint.
Download links for exports/documents expire and are audited (BP §14.4; values D-152).

### 1.13 Files (M22)
Uploads go through API-M22-01 (type/size restrictions, malware scan for risky attachments, data class
public/private) and are referenced by `file_id` in business requests. Private files are served only through
authorised access (API-M22-02) (BP §19.1). URL-based media import is SSRF-restricted (API-M22-04; BP §7.4, §19.1).

### 1.14 Audit
Every material change (stock, price, refund, permission, approval, catalog publication, configuration) writes
E-audit_event with actor, time, object, action, before/after (or structured change) and reason (BP §17.3, §17.6,
§20.1). Endpoints marked `Aud` in `05-backend.md` rules must reject material overrides without a reason.

### 1.15 Catalogue notation
- **Auth·Authz cell** = class · `permission.key`: roles with their `07-auth-roles-permissions.md` §5 cell codes
  (`As` assigned, `Loc` locations, `Own`/`Org`, `Rq` request only, `≤T` within threshold, `Ap` approves, `M`/`C`
  maker/checker, `D:D-xxx` decided by) · scope · Idem. It is generated from 07 §5/§13 (2026-09-27); guards (MFA,
  step-up, SoD, reason) are listed in 07 §13. Keys marked "(new key — add to 07)" are proposed here for endpoints
  added in §8 and must be registered in 07 before implementation (07 §13.24: no key → denied).
- **Columns:** ID · Endpoint · Purpose · Auth·Authz · Request (params; body) ·
  Validation · Response · Errors · Entities · Service (backend module/service, names defined in `05-backend.md`) ·
  UI (frontend consumers) · Source (evidence label + citation) · Status.
- UI codes: `P-S##`, `P-E##`, `P-V##` from `00-conventions.md` §8; `shell(S)`, `shell(E)`, `shell(V)` = the
  storefront / ERP / vendor shells in `assets/tradex.js`; `#tab` / `m-…` = mockup tab or modal id.
- `L` = list parameters (§1.9); `std` = standard errors (§1.7); `?` = optional field; `[]` = array.
- All entities used are in the registry (`00-conventions.md` §7, §7.1, §7.2); see §5 for the accepted additions.
- **Status rule:** D-001 (operational core), D-079, D-080, D-083 are prerequisites of the API layer as a whole and
  are tracked on the M01/M02 foundation tasks; they are **not repeated per endpoint**. Per-endpoint status is
  `REQUIRES_DECISION (D-xxx)` only when an endpoint-specific decision is OPEN; otherwise `NOT_STARTED`.
- Endpoints marked "→ §4.x" have an expanded contract in §4 (Detailed contracts).

## 3. Endpoint catalogue (by module)

Columns and notation: §1.15. Modules M01, M15, M25, M26, M28 and M29 expose no external endpoints in Phase 1 (M15/M28/M29 are LATER; M26 data is surfaced through API-M24-08).

### 3.1 M02 — Identity, access & audit

(D-083) | not expired; attempts ≤ cap; purpose-bound | sign_in → {session (D-083), principal}; other purposes → {verification_token, expires_at} | 422 CODE_INVALID, 410 CHALLENGE_EXPIRED, 429 | E-verification_challenge, E-user_account, E-user_session, E-audit_event | M02 OtpService, SessionService | P-S12#signin, P-S07, P-S09#profile, P-V04#terms, P-S14, P-E16, P-V05 | MOCKUP (MK:store-login.html#signin) | REQUIRES_DECISION (D-040) |

---

## 4. Detailed contracts

Expanded contracts for the endpoints marked "→ §4.x" in §3. Field lists are logical; names/types are finalised with
`03-database.md` and D-080.

### 4.1 API-M04-03 · `GET /v1/products/{product_id}/offers` — context-priced offers (DOCUMENTED BP §17.4)
- **Auth/authz:** PUB or CUS. Price context derived only from the session (BP §8.4): guest/consumer → public list;
  dealer → only if verified member of an approved business account (BP §6.5); contract price if approved for that
  account (BP §8.1 step 2). Response `Cache-Control: private, no-store` whenever context ≠ public (T22).
- **Request:** path `product_id`; query `sku_id?`, `quantity?` (tier preview), `pin?` (delivery estimate).
- **Validation:** only approved, published offers (BP §17.4, §17.6 "Unapproved vendor content cannot become a
  purchasable offer"); client-sent segment/price fields ignored (T10).
- **Response 200:** `offers[{offer_id, sku_id, seller{type: company_internal, display_name}, condition{grade,
  label, meaning}, unit_ref? (unique refurbished unit), price{amount, tax_basis (D-016), reference_price?, savings?},
  tiers[{min_qty, unit_price}]? (dealer only), availability{message, source (company_stock,
  supplier_confirmation_required), lead_time_days?, expected_restock_date? (only with a disclosed-lead-time policy,
  D-073), as_of}, delivery{serviceable?, estimate?}, warranty{provider,
  duration, policy_version}, returns{window, policy_version}, context{segment, price_list_ref (opaque)}}]`.
  Availability is a projection, not a promise (BP §9.1, §16.5); supplier stock is labelled and never shown as
  company stock (BP §9.7). No offer for a context → `offers: []` (not an error).
- **Errors:** `404` product not published; `std`.
- **Tests:** T01, T02, T10, T14, T22.

### 4.2 API-M05-01 · `POST /v1/quotes` — quote a basket (DOCUMENTED BP §17.4, §8.1)
- **Auth/authz:** PUB, CUS, GAT, LNK, or STF (assisted, on behalf of a selected customer context). Context resolved
  server-side (BP §8.1 step 1).
- **Request body:** `items[{offer_id, quantity}]` or `cart:true` or `draft_order_id`; `delivery{pin | address_id,
  method?}`; `coupon_codes[]?` (D-043); `billing{use_gst_invoice?, gstin?}`; `previous_quote_id?`; `channel`
  (validated against entry point, BP §17.5).
- **Processing (BP §8.1, proposed sequence D-017):** 1 identify company, currency, channel, offer, customer context;
  2 contract → dealer list → public list; 3 quantity tier for SKU/offer & UoM (D-018); 4 eligible promotions under
  explicit stacking policy (D-043) — never "largest discount wins" unless approved; 5 shipping and tax (D-016);
  6 margin/discount authority and serviceability; 7 lock a time-limited quote version (validity window D-154).
  Expired price list → configured fallback or checkout blocked, never zero (BP §8.4).
- **Validation:** quantities > 0; offers approved and visible to context; coupon validity (D-043); location change
  re-evaluates serviceability, tax and location price (BP §8.4; D-044).
- **Response 201:** `{quote_id, version, expires_at, context{segment, price_list_ref}, lines[{line_ref, offer_id,
  sku_id, qty, unit_price, tier_applied?, promotions[], discount_allocation, tax{rate_ref, amount}, line_total,
  rule_version, notices[] (price_changed_since_previous, qty_capped, supplier_confirmation_needed,
  split_shipment)}], shipping{method, charge}, totals{subtotal, discounts, shipping, tax, total, tax_basis},
  material_changes[]? (vs previous_quote_id → must be accepted), advisory_availability[{line_ref, available}],
  serviceable}`. A quote **does not reserve stock** (BP §10.2).
- **Errors:** `422 VALIDATION_FAILED`; `422 NOT_SERVICEABLE`; `403 CONTEXT_INELIGIBLE`; `422 COUPON_INVALID`
  (D-043); `409 CHECKOUT_BLOCKED_PRICE_LIST` (expired list with block fallback); `std`.
- **Entities:** E-quote, E-quote_line, E-price_list, E-price_list_item, E-quantity_tier, E-promotion,
  E-price_rule_version, E-margin_floor. **Tests:** T02, T03, T10, T22, T33.

### 4.3 API-M10-06 · `POST /v1/orders` — place pending order (DOCUMENTED BP §17.4, §17.5, §10.2)
- **Auth/authz:** CUS (consumer; dealer member with ordering permission and per-order limit, D-066); guest only if
  D-021 allows (with verified phone token); LNK (checkout link for a draft order). **Idem: required** (D-079).
- **Request body (BP §17.5 example extended):** `quote_id`, `quote_version`, `items[{offer_id, quantity}]` (must
  equal the quote), `shipping_address_id` or `guest_address{…}` (D-021), `billing{use_gst_invoice, gstin?,
  business_name?}` (dealer billing verified & locked), `delivery{method (standard, express, pickup — D-061),
  pickup_location_id?, instructions?}`, `shipment_preference?` (ship_available_now, ship_together — D-029),
  `policy_acknowledgements[{policy_type, version}]`, `channel`, `draft_order_id?`, `order_approval_id?`
  (API-M08-48), `contact{email?, verification_token?}`. When the order is placed is governed by D-161 (the mockup
  starts the reservation countdown at checkout step 1; BP §16.3 requires reservation and pending order together).
- **Processing:**
  1. Derive buyer context; check address ownership, quote scope (same context), version and expiry (BP §17.5).
  2. Recompute totals (BP §10.2 step 1). Material difference → `409 PRICE_CHANGED` with a new quote for explicit
     acceptance (BP §6.3 "price changed", §8.1 step 8).
  3. Validate offer approval, serviceability, eligibility (membership, order limit), policy acknowledgements.
  4. **One stock-authority transaction** (BP §16.3): create E-sales_order (`awaiting_payment`) with line snapshots
     (price, rule version, condition, warranty/return policy versions — BP §8.1, §17.6, T33) **and** reservations
     for every line (specific serial when a unique unit is advertised, else quantity — D-031) with expiry (D-026).
     All lines reserve or none do.
  5. Write E-idempotency_record, E-audit_event and E-outbox_operation (order_created); no customer confirmation is
     sent before payment verification (BP §6.3).
- **Response 201 (replay → same body):** `{order_id, order_number, order_state: "awaiting_payment", payment_state:
  "not_started", reservation{expires_at, lines[{line_id, reserved_qty, unit_ref?}]}, totals{…}, lines[{line_id,
  sku_id, condition, qty, unit_price, discount_allocation, tax, line_total, price_rule_version,
  policy_versions{warranty, returns}}], next{action: "create_payment_attempt"}}`. COD (only if D-020 enabled) →
  `order_state: confirmed`, `payment_state: cod_due`.
- **Errors:** `409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT` · `409 STOCK_UNAVAILABLE{lines[{line_ref, requested, available}]}` (T04;
  dealer shortage alternatives BP §29.2: reduce quantity, request quote, reject) · `409 PRICE_CHANGED{new_quote}` ·
  `410 QUOTE_EXPIRED` · `422 NOT_SERVICEABLE` · `403 CONTEXT_INELIGIBLE` · `403 ORDER_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` (D-066; the buyer can submit the quote for owner approval via API-M08-46 and then
  place it with `order_approval_id`) ·
  `422 POLICY_ACK_REQUIRED` · `503 STOCK_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE` (BP §10.3) · `std`.
- **Tests:** T04, T05, T06, T10, T21, T33.

### 4.4 API-M10-07 · `GET /v1/orders/{order_id}` — read order (DOCUMENTED BP §17.4)
- **Auth/authz:** CUS own order or own business account (member visibility D-066); GAT scoped to this order; STF by
  role and location scope. Others → `404`.
- **Response 200:** `{order_id, order_number, placed_at, channel, buyer{display, contact_masked}, order_state,
  payment{state, captured_amount, refunded_amount, attempts_summary[]}, lines[{line_id, sku_id, title_snapshot,
  condition_snapshot, unit_ref?, qty, unit_price, discount, tax, line_total, price_rule_version,
  policy_versions, line_state (open, cancel_requested, cancelled, dispatched, delivered, returned), cancellable,
  returnable}], fulfilments[{fulfilment_id, state, ship_from, lines[], carrier, awb?, tracking_link?,
  promise, proof_of_delivery? (carrier evidence; delivery OTP per D-183), events[{at, canonical_state,
  raw_status (STF only)}]}], returns[{rma_id, state}], refunds[{refund_id,
  state, amount}], invoices[{invoice_id, number}], address (masked for GAT), allowed_actions[] (pay,
  cancel_lines, request_return, download_invoice), staff{price_snapshot, reservation{expires_at, lines[]},
  payment_events[], timeline[], audit[], notes[], holds[], open_exceptions[]}? (STF only)}`. Order, payment, fulfilment and return
  states are separate fields (BP §10.1). `Cache-Control: private, no-store`.
- **Errors:** `404`, `std`. **Tests:** T11-style object-level checks, T23 (no disclosure without access), T33.

### 4.5 API-M10-09 · `POST /v1/orders/{order_id}/cancellations` — cancel eligible lines (DOCUMENTED BP §17.4)
- **Auth/authz:** CUS own/org, GAT, STF (R-sales_support+, loc). **Idem: required.**
- **Request body:** `lines[{line_id, quantity}]`, `reason_code` (customer_request, out_of_stock, pricing_error,
  suspected_fraud — last three staff only), `sub_reason?` (customer reason catalogue, D-139), `note?`, `dry_run?`.
- **Preview (`dry_run: true`):** no effect; returns per-line eligibility, allocated refund (line discount and tax
  allocation, BP §8.4), reservation/stock effect and promotion effect (D-082) — used by P-E02 m-cancel "Refund
  preview" and P-S08.
- **Validation (BP §17.4 "State, quantity and refund rules"):** line not dispatched (customers are pointed to
  returns after dispatch); quantity ≤ open quantity; supplier-shipment lines cancellable while pending (MK
  store-order); staff reasons require permission; tier/discount effect of partial cancellation → **D-082**.
- **Effects (one core transaction):** E-order_cancellation; release **only** affected reservations (BP §10.3);
  recompute order state; if captured → one E-refund request for the allocated line amount using original line
  discount and tax allocation (BP §8.4, §17.6); outbox: fulfilment update (line set aside), customer notification.
- **Response 201:** `{cancellation_id, lines[{line_id, cancelled_qty}], order_state, reservations_released[],
  refund{refund_id, amount, state}?}`.
- **Errors:** `409 STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID` (already dispatched), `422 POLICY_INELIGIBLE`, `409
  IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`, `std`. **Tests:** T34, T06.

### 4.6 API-M07-09 / API-M07-13 · goods receipt (DOCUMENTED BP §17.4 "Receive goods")
- **Auth/authz:** STF R-warehouse_staff or R-branch_manager assigned to the receiving location (BP §17.4 "Warehouse
  permission"; §18.1 "Record matching receipt"). **Idem: required** for create-with-post and posting.
- **API-M07-09 request:** `{po_id, location_id, supplier_reference, vehicle_no?, dock?, lines[{po_line_id,
  qty_received, serials[]?, handling?}]?, post: false|true}`.
- **Validation:** PO approved/sent and not closed; lines belong to PO (PO match); serial format, uniqueness
  enterprise-wide, not already in stock (BP §17.4 "serial validation", §9.4); over-receipt and substitutions →
  held pending approval (BP §9.3).
- **API-M07-13 posting request:** `{expected_version, confirm_variances: true}`.
- **Posting effects (one stock transaction, BP §9.2):** received quantity → received/quarantine disposition; units
  with QC `pass` (API-M06-09) → sellable ("Inspection accepted"); QC `fail` → stay in quarantine with supplier
  resolution (E-supplier_rma draft); wrong/duplicate serial or not-on-PO → E-exception_case, **no stock** (T15);
  PO received quantities/status updated (partial/received); outbox: availability refresh (A04), bill matching
  (§9.3, A25); audit. A PO issue itself never changes on-hand (BP §9.2).
- **Response:** `{receipt_id, grn_number, status (draft, posted), lines[], variances[], movements[{sku, qty,
  to_disposition}], units{sellable, quarantined, held}, po_status, exceptions[]}`.
- **Errors:** `409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`, `409 STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID` (already posted), `422 UNRESOLVED_LINES`,
  `409 SERIAL_CONFLICT`, `std`. **Tests:** T15, T21, T26-style repeat safety.

### 4.7 API-M11-01 / -02 / -03 · payment attempt, status, webhook (DOCUMENTED BP §10.2, §17.4)
**API-M11-01 `POST /v1/orders/{order_id}/payment-attempts`** (Idem required)
- Request `{method_preference (upi, card, netbanking, emi — D-062, cod — D-020, bank_transfer — D-012/D-019),
  client_return_ref}`. Amount is always the server's order payable.
- Guards: order `awaiting_payment`; reservation active — if expired, attempt controlled re-reservation first, else
  `409 RESERVATION_EXPIRED` (checkout "Re-check & reserve again"); reuse the current attempt if still valid (BP §10.2
  step 3); card data never handled by the application (BP §10.6).
- Effects: E-payment_attempt `created` + outbox operation; provider order/session created outside the DB
  transaction; timeout → attempt `pending` and reconciliation (BP §16.3).
- Response 201 `{attempt_id, attempt_state, amount, provider_session{type (redirect, sdk_params) per D-012},
  expires_at}`; bank transfer → `{instructions{beneficiary_masked, reference}}`.
- Errors `409 RESERVATION_EXPIRED`, `409 STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID`, `422 METHOD_NOT_AVAILABLE`, `503
  PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, `409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`.

**API-M11-02 `GET /v1/orders/{order_id}/payment-status`**
- Response `{order_state, payment_state, attempt{attempt_id, state, method, public_failure_reason?},
  order_confirmed, next_action (wait, retry_payment, contact_support), checked_with_provider_at}`. The page shows
  "verification in progress" while pending — never a false success (BP §6.3).

**API-M11-03 `POST /v1/webhooks/payment-provider`**
1. Verify signature on the raw body → failure `401 SIGNATURE_INVALID` (security log only).
2. Deduplicate by provider event id → duplicate returns `200 {result: duplicate}` (T07).
3. Store E-payment_event (type, provider refs, raw payload ref, signature result, received_at).
4. Apply legal transitions on E-payment_attempt: created/pending → authorised/captured/failed/expired; captured is
   never downgraded (`result: ignored_out_of_order`, T08).
5. On capture: if order `awaiting_payment` and reservation active → order `confirmed`, reservation becomes
   confirmed allocation, outbox → fulfilment release (A05/A11), customer notification (A14), invoice process
   (D-055). If reservation expired → controlled re-reservation; success → confirm; failure → order `on_hold` +
   E-exception_case "payment captured without stock", resolve/refund per policy (BP §10.3, §29.6; D-026; T09).
6. Amount/currency mismatch or unknown attempt → stored + E-exception_case (payment review).
7. Refund events update E-refund (pending → completed/failed).
- Response `200 {result: applied | duplicate | ignored_out_of_order | exception}` quickly; heavy work via outbox.
- **Tests:** T06, T07, T08, T09, T21.

### 4.8 API-M06-23 · `POST /v1/stock-adjustments` (DOCUMENTED BP §9.6, §18.1)
- **Auth/authz:** STF R-warehouse_staff (request), R-branch_manager/R-ops_admin (request; approve within threshold
  via API-M17-03), R-finance (value review), R-owner (high value). **Idem: required.**
- **Request:** `{location_id, bin_id?, sku_id, unit_ids[]?, qty_delta (signed), from_disposition?,
  to_disposition?, reason_code (count_variance, damage, loss, found, correction), source_ref (count_line_id,
  transfer_id, fulfilment_id)?, evidence_file_ids[], note}`.
- **Validation:** reason mandatory; adjustments are movements — never overwrite a quantity (BP §9.6); value computed
  server-side; would-be negative sellable allocation with active reservations → exception (BP §17.6).
- **Routing:** staff submit, managers approve within threshold, large unexplained losses to owner/finance (BP §9.6,
  §18.1); thresholds by quantity and value are client-supplied (D-024); requester cannot approve own request where
  feasible (BP §18.2).
- **Response:** `202 {adjustment_id, approval_request_id, required_approver_level, value}`; on approval the movement
  is posted with reason and approver (audit) and the availability projection refreshes (A04).
- **Errors:** `422 REASON_REQUIRED`, `409 STOCK_NEGATIVE`, `409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`, `std`. **Tests:** T31, A24 run.

### 4.9 API-M11-10 / -12 · refund transitions and safe retries (DOCUMENTED BP §10.1, §10.3)
- **API-M11-10 request:** `{action (approve, submit, check_release, hold, release_hold), reason?, expected_version}`.
- **Guards:** approve — approver authority for amount (D-024), approver ≠ requester (BP §18.2); submit — approved,
  amount ≤ refundable (captured − completed − pending) (BP §17.6); provider call carries the refund's deterministic
  idempotency reference (T18); intent recorded in outbox before the external call (BP §16.3); timeout → `pending`
  + `202 PROVIDER_PENDING`; check_release — second finance user for bank-transfer route (maker ≠ checker).
- **API-M11-12 request:** `{route (original_method, new_upi_verified, bank_transfer), route_details_ref?,
  status_check_ref}` — `status_check_ref` must come from a recent API-M11-11 call saying `safe_to_retry`
  (BP §10.3 "Query existing refund status before issuing another"; T19). New attempt key = refund id + attempt no.
- **Response:** `{refund_id, state, attempt_no?, provider_ref?}`. **Errors:** `409 STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID`, `403
  AUTHORITY_EXCEEDED`, `403 SELF_APPROVAL_NOT_ALLOWED`, `422 AMOUNT_EXCEEDS_REFUNDABLE`, `409
  STATUS_CHECK_REQUIRED`, `202 PROVIDER_PENDING`. **Tests:** T18, T19, T27, T34.

### 4.10 API-M12-11 / -12 / -15 / -16 · courier booking, dispatch, carrier events (DOCUMENTED BP §10.4–10.5, §17.4)
- **API-M12-11 request:** `{parcels[{fulfilment_id, parcel_ref, weight, dimensions?, service_level?}]}`; guards:
  packed and serial-verified; serviceable; `parcel_ref` is the booking idempotency reference with the carrier;
  after any timeout a status query (API-M12-12) precedes re-booking (T20). Response `202 {bookings[{parcel_ref,
  state (requested, booked, failed, needs_reconciliation), awb?, label_ready}]}`; persistent failure → exception
  and manual fallback (API-M12-13; A38).
- **API-M12-12 response:** `{parcel_ref, state, awb?, carrier_lookup (found_existing, not_found), linked}` —
  an existing AWB found at the carrier is linked, never duplicated.
- **API-M12-15 request:** `{courier, rider_name, pickup_otp? (MOCKUP), scanned_parcel_refs[], evidence_file_id?}`.
  Only scanned parcels become dispatched; others stay ready (MK). Per parcel, one stock transaction: fulfilment →
  `dispatched`; E-stock_movement reduces on-hand and consumes the reservation atomically (BP §9.2); serial event
  links the dispatched serial and inspection record to the sale (BP §10.4); a serial cannot ship twice (BP §17.6).
  Outbox: customer notification (A14), tracking, invoice/accounting per D-055. Response `{manifest_id,
  dispatched[], not_dispatched[{parcel_ref, reason}]}`; errors `409 SERIAL_ALREADY_SHIPPED`, `409
  STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID`, `409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`.
- **API-M12-16 webhook:** provider authentication (D-013); dedupe by provider event id (or AWB + status + time);
  store raw event (BP §10.5 "preserve the raw event"); map carrier status → canonical (in_transit,
  out_for_delivery, delivered, delivery_failed, rto_initiated, returned_to_origin, lost_damaged) via a
  configuration mapping; apply only legal fulfilment transitions and never overwrite unrelated order/payment/return
  states (BP §10.5); `delivered` starts return/warranty windows (PR2 §6); `delivery_failed`/RTO → exception queue.
  Response `200`. **Tests:** T07-style duplicate event, T20, T21.

### 4.11 API-M13-03 / -10 / -11 · return request, receipt, decisions (DOCUMENTED BP §17.4, §10.5)
- **API-M13-03 request:** `{order_id | store_invoice_number, lines[{order_line_id, qty, unit_ref?}], type
  (return_refund, replacement, warranty_repair, doa), reason_code, since_when?, description, serial_entered?,
  serial_verification_ref?, evidence_file_ids[], condition_checklist[{item, confirmed}], return_method{type
  (pickup, store_drop), slot?, location_id?}, refund_destination{type (original_method, bank_account — COD only,
  D-020)}, policy_acknowledgement{policy_id, version}, personal_data_removed_confirmed}`. **Idem: required.**
- **Guards:** order access (BP §17.4); per-line eligibility against the **policy version stored on the order**
  (window, condition, category, DOA window — D-022) → `422 POLICY_INELIGIBLE{lines[]}`; line/serial match (BP
  §17.4) — a mismatch is shown to the customer (BP §6.3 state) and the request can only proceed flagged for staff
  review; no automatic accusation or rejection (A37). Effects: E-return_request `requested` with policy snapshot
  (MK), A22 routing to a review queue, acknowledgement. Response `201 {rma_id, rma_number, state, next_steps[],
  policy_snapshot}`.
- **API-M13-10 request:** `{scan_ref (RMA number, parcel label, AWB), source (rma, rto), location_id,
  units[{unit_id | serial, condition_note}], photo_file_ids[]?}` (Idem). Guards: RMA authorised/in transit, or RTO
  fulfilment; serial matched to the original sale (T17); unknown parcel → exception. Effects: movement to
  **quarantine** (never sellable, BP §9.2, §10.5), RMA `received`, serial event, storage devices flagged for erasure
  (BP §7.5). Response `{rma_id?, state, units[{unit_id, disposition: quarantine, serial_match}]}`.
- **API-M13-11 request:** `{decision_type (refund, disposition, replacement), refund{action (approve, hold),
  lines[{line_id, amount}], reason}?, disposition{outcome (resale, repair, supplier_rma, scrap_write_off,
  return_to_customer), grade_inspection_ref?, reason}?, replacement{lines[]}?}`. Guards: refund and resale are
  separate decisions (BP §10.5); resale only after completed inspection (and erasure for storage devices, BP §7.5);
  refund authority per BP §18.1 (finance executes/approves as assigned); write-off thresholds (D-024). Effects:
  refund → E-refund via M11 (idempotent, T18); resale → quarantine → sellable movement ("Return approved for
  resale", BP §9.2); supplier_rma → draft; replacement → API-M10-23; RMA `resolved` when all decisions complete.
  Response `{rma_state, refund?, movement_id?, supplier_rma_id?, replacement_order_id?}`.
- **Tests:** T17, T18, T19, T33, T34.

### 4.12 API-M14-06 / API-M14-19 · vendor submission and availability update (DOCUMENTED BP §17.4, §11.2–11.3)
- **API-M14-06 request:** `{type (new_product, change), listing_id?, category_id, condition_type, brand_id, model,
  mpn, vendor_code, tax_classification_ref, barcode?, attributes{}, grade, warranty{provider, duration},
  defects_notes, inspection_evidence{checklist_ref, date}, erasure_evidence?, media_file_ids[],
  image_rights_confirmed, description, accessories[], supplier_price{amount, valid_until},
  availability{supplier_qty, lead_time_days, dispatch_location_id}, min_order_qty?,
  supplier_fulfilment_eligible?, submit}`.
- **Guards:** vendor id from session only — client `seller_id` ignored (BP §17.4); category within permitted scope
  (BP §11.1) → `403 CATEGORY_NOT_PERMITTED`; suspended vendor → `403 VENDOR_SUSPENDED` (BP §11.1); category
  schema required attributes; warranty provider mandatory (BP §29.3); image rights (BP §7.4); sensitive fields
  (brand, condition, warranty, tax, extraordinary price change) flagged for review (BP §11.3); **never
  publishes** — approval (API-M17-03) and publication checks are separate (BP §17.4, T12, T13).
- **API-M14-06 response:** `201 {submission_id, state (draft, submitted), validation{errors[], warnings[]},
  sensitive_fields[]}`.
- **API-M14-19 request:** `{source (portal, csv, api), file_id?, offers[{offer_ref | vendor_code, supplier_qty,
  lead_time_days, confirm_unchanged?}], confirm_all_unchanged?}` (Idem). Guards: own approved offers only; bounds
  from configuration; routine refreshes auto-accepted within validated boundaries once the workflow is stable,
  others routed to review (BP §11.3); a stock update never creates company-owned inventory (BP §11.3); freshness
  clock reset with timestamp and source (BP §9.7; deadline D-028).
- **API-M14-19 response:** `200 {update_id, accepted[], review_required[], rejected[{row, reason}],
  freshness{state, next_deadline}}`. **Tests:** T11, T12, T13, T14.

### 4.13 API-M17-03 · `POST /v1/approvals/{approval_request_id}/decision` (DOCUMENTED BP §17.4)
- **Request:** `{decision (approve, reject, request_changes, approve_and_publish, counter), reason (mandatory for
  reject, request_changes, overrides and sensitive approvals — BP §18.2 "Record why"), fields_needing_changes[]?,
  counter_value? (discount overrides), keep_live_version_until_resubmit? (vendor content), flag_recurring? (BP
  §12.6), notify_affected_account_managers? (price-list changes, MK m-decide), expected_version (BP §17.4
  "expected version")}`. The requester is always notified.
- **Guards:** caller authority for the approval type and value (thresholds D-024, active delegation D-025,
  emergency access); not the requester where separation applies (BP §18.2); approval pending and not expired;
  `expected_version` equals the subject version under review (prevents approving a stale change).
- **Effects:** decision + audit; dispatch to the owning module — catalog publish (M04; publication checks still
  apply), vendor submission (M14; pending versions never overwrite live until approved, BP §11.3), price-list
  version activation (M05), stock adjustment posting (M06), PO approval (M07), refund approval (M11), payout
  change (M14 maker-checker), threshold/configuration/role change (M17/M24/M02); requester notified.
- **Response:** `200 {approval_request_id, status, subject{type, id, new_state}}`.
- **Errors:** `403 AUTHORITY_EXCEEDED`, `403 SELF_APPROVAL_NOT_ALLOWED`, `409 VERSION_CONFLICT`, `409
  STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID`, `422 REASON_REQUIRED`. **Tests:** T12, T13, T31.

### 4.14 API-M10-10 / -11 · guest order access (DOCUMENTED BP §6.5, §13.4; method D-021)
- **API-M10-10 request:** `{order_number, phone}`; rate-limited (D-084). If the pair matches, an OTP goes to the
  phone on the order. Response is always `202 {challenge_id, expires_at}` (no enumeration).
- **API-M10-11 request:** `{code}` → `200 {order_access_token (GAT), scope: [read, pay, cancel_lines,
  request_return, download_invoice], order_id, expires_at}`. Errors `422 CODE_INVALID`, `410 CHALLENGE_EXPIRED`,
  `429`. Token lifetime and whether a secure link replaces OTP → D-021. **Test:** T23.

### 4.15 API-M10-18 / -19 / -20 / -22 · assisted orders and secure checkout links (DOCUMENTED BP §13.1–13.2, §29.5)
- **API-M10-18/-19 request:** `{source (whatsapp_basket, phone, walk_in), conversation_id?, customer{customer_id |
  business_account_id | new_walk_in{name, mobile}}, fulfil_from{location_id | pickup_location_id}, lines[{sku_id |
  offer_id, qty}], staff_discount{percent, reason_code (bulk_tender, price_match_evidence, service_recovery),
  evidence_file_id?}?, expected_version? (-19)}`.
- **Guards:** acting role from the session — the mockup "Acting as" selector is a prototype aid (BP §17.4
  `is_admin` never trusted); one price calculation for staff and WhatsApp orders (BP §8.1); discount above the
  caller's authority → approval request (BP §18.1; limits D-024); ambiguous chat items must be resolved to an
  explicit SKU/condition by the agent (BP §13.2); no stock reserved at draft (MK).
- **Response:** `201/200 {draft_order_id, lines[{…, unit_price, rule}], totals, discount{status
  (within_authority, approval_required), approval_request_id?}, suggested_fulfil_from (single location that can
  fulfil every line, BP §9.5; D-029)}`.
- **API-M10-20 request:** `{channel (whatsapp_template, sms, email), link_expiry_option}` (Idem). Guards: consent and
  WhatsApp service window/approved template (BP §13.3); no pending approvals on the draft; longer expiry needs
  approval (MK); **reservation timing at send vs at customer confirmation → D-151**. Response `201 {link_id,
  expires_at, sent_via, reservation?{expires_at}}`.
- **API-M10-22:** the link opens the draft; price is recomputed (BP §13.2 "A product reference from chat is not a
  trusted price"); the customer confirms address and final price and pays through API-M10-06 → API-M11-01 with
  the same controls as web checkout (BP §29.5). **Tests:** T10, T23, T24, T25.

### 4.16 API-M16-10 · `POST /v1/webhooks/whatsapp` (DOCUMENTED BP §13.3; provider D-014)
Provider signature → dedupe by message id → store E-integration_event → inbound message: find/create the
conversation by WhatsApp identity (no account merge without verified link, BP §13.4); refresh the 24-hour service
window (BP §13.3); opt-out keywords → E-consent_record; if automation not paused → guided step (A08 draft basket in
1B, A15 verified order status); ambiguity or failure → human handoff with context (T24). Delivery statuses update
message/notification delivery state (failures visible). Response `200`.

### 4.17 API-M21-01 · `GET /v1/products` — search catalog (DOCUMENTED BP §17.4, §6.4)
- **Request:** `q`, `category_id`, `filters[attribute_key]=values` (only attributes of the category schema,
  BP §6.4), `condition[]`, `brand[]`, `price_min/max` (context price), `rating_min` (D-041), `in_stock_only` /
  `include_out_of_stock`, `sort` (relevance, price_asc, price_desc, newest, rating — D-041, biggest_discount),
  `deals` (products on an approved promotion — D-043, D-142), `exact` (disable spelling correction/synonyms for this
  request), `page`, `page_size`. Staff only (assisted orders): `on_behalf_of{customer_id | business_account_id}`
  (context price for that buyer) and `include=location_availability`.
- **Processing:** only the approved visible catalog (BP §17.4); exact model/SKU match, spelling variants,
  abbreviations and curated synonyms (BP §6.4; D-032 engine); results are a discovery projection, not stock
  authority (BP §16.5); zero-result searches logged anonymously (BP §4).
- **Response:** `{items[{product_id, sku_id?, title, image, key_specs[], condition{grade, label}, price{amount,
  tax_basis, reference_price?}, tier_hint? (verified dealers only — guests never get private prices, BP §6.4),
  warranty_summary, stock_message{text, as_of}, expected_restock_date? (D-073), rating? (only real data),
  unit_ref?, location_availability[]? (staff)}], facets[{key, label,
  values[{value, count}]}], total, spelling{corrected_query?, synonyms_applied[]}, zero_result{suggestions[],
  alternatives[]}?}`. Guest responses cacheable; dealer responses `private`.
- **Errors:** `std`. **Tests:** T01, T02, T22, T36.

## 5. Registry additions (status 2026-09-27)

All entities requested by the first version of this file were **accepted** into `00-conventions.md` §7.1
(E-verification_challenge, E-user_session, E-invitation, E-access_review, E-api_credential, E-terms_acceptance,
E-data_request, E-internal_note, E-cart/E-cart_line, E-wishlist_item, E-product_review, E-product_question,
E-alert_subscription, E-customer_tag, E-promotion_code, E-merch_collection, E-import_mapping_profile,
E-search_synonym, E-seo_redirect, E-pick_wave, E-handover_manifest, E-advance_shipping_notice, E-finance_day_close,
E-saved_view, E-change_request), with their build conditions (e.g. E-pick_wave/E-handover_manifest MOCKUP-ONLY
D-132, E-internal_note D-134, E-customer_merge/E-registered_device D-133). Second-wave entities of §7.2 are used in
the catalogue: E-verification_document (API-M08-20/-22/-39/-50, API-M14-01/-33/-35/-49/-66…-68),
E-payout_account_change (API-M14-33/-43/-44/-59/-69), E-notification_preference (API-M20-08/-09),
E-fulfilment_parcel / E-fulfilment_scan (API-M12-04/-07/-09…-15), E-cost_signal (API-M05-22/-23), E-device_station
(API-M06-10, API-M12-09/-21), E-vendor_announcement (API-M14-03), E-landed_cost_charge (API-M07-11),
E-supplier_confirmation (API-M14-63), E-shipping_charge_rule (API-M05-01, API-M12-01), E-restore_rehearsal
(API-M24-09/-11). The accepted relationship clarifications (reverse shipments on E-fulfilment, vendor counter-party
on E-support_conversation, single E-exception_case type) are relied on by API-M12-18, API-M13-09, API-M14-11/-53/-60.

Open for the database owner (from this file's gap resolution): E-approval_request needs a customer-side type for
business-account order approvals (API-M08-46…48; 07 entity gap E-01) and the approval types listed in 07 entity gap
E-02 plus `terms_publication`, `document_template`, `report_definition`, `tax_classification`, `policy_version`,
`integration_credential`, `business_account_change` used by the endpoints added in §8.

## 6. Proposed new decisions

All decisions below are now in `DECISIONS.md` (OPEN). **Merged 2026-09-27:** D-140 → D-129, D-143 → D-121, D-144 → D-131, D-148 → D-137 (and, from other files, D-120 → D-104, D-180 → D-164, D-182 → D-162); this file uses only the surviving IDs. D-150 is proposed in `05-backend.md`. Related proposals by other plan files that this file does **not** duplicate: pick waves/manifests as records (D-132), customer merge & device registration (D-133), staff internal notes (D-134) — all in `03-database.md`; money representation (D-104, `01-tech-stack.md`). Endpoints API-M12-05/-14, API-M08-15/-43, API-M08-30/API-M10-16 depend on those if they are adopted.

| ID | Question | Documented options / proposal (source) | Blocks |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-141 | Which **storefront engagement extras** are built: back-in-stock / price-drop / saved-search alerts, "Request this product" sourcing, "Report a spec issue", newsletter subscription, wishlist share link? | Not in BP/PR (MOCKUP-ONLY): MK:store-listing.html, store-product.html, store-cart.html, store-refurbished.html, store-account.html#wishlist, assets/tradex.js footer. Related consent rules BP §13.3; abandoned-cart reminders are P2 (A29). Options: launch / later / not built | API-M04-14, API-M08-11, API-M08-19, API-M16-11 (sourcing), API-M20-06/07 |
| D-142 | What is the **source and editor for merchandising content** (home curated collections, deals of the day, banners, "popular right now") and vendor-portal announcements? | BP §6.3 Home "curated collections, trust information"; BP §15.6 "curated recommendations before a recommendation model"; MK:store-home.html, vendor-dashboard.html "Announcements & policy updates"; no ERP screen in mockup. Options: catalog-admin screen, content in storefront code, other | API-M09-01, API-M14-03 (announcements), E-merch_collection |
| D-145 | Build **CRM extras**: customer tags and staff-initiated consented bulk messages? | Not documented (MOCKUP-ONLY MK:erp-customers.html "Add tag", "Send consented message"); consent/template rules BP §13.3, D-058 | API-M08-31, API-M08-32 |
| D-146 | **Live-update mechanism** for shared inbox, work queues, scan station, notification bell and payment-status page: polling interval vs server push? | Not specified; BP §13.3 shared inbox, §10.2 server-verified payment status; MK:erp-support.html, erp-fulfilment.html; technology must follow D-001/D-002 | API-M16-04, API-M16-06, API-M20-01, API-M11-02 |
| D-147 | Which **warehouse/counter devices** are integrated (label & invoice printers, weighing scale, bench camera, scanners, data-erasure station, POS card terminals) and how? | BP §10.4 scan + print invoice/packing label; BP §7.2/§7.5 data-erasure evidence; MK:erp-fulfilment.html (printer, scale SC-01, bench camera), erp-returns.html (erasure station), erp-orders.html m-assisted (card terminal) — device names are samples | API-M06-10 (station), API-M12-09 (weight/photo), API-M03-07/API-M12-10 print path |
| D-149 | Staff **"view as dealer"**: read-only preview (price simulator) or session impersonation with audit? | MK:erp-customers.html "View as dealer"; BP §18.2 (emergency access, least privilege), §19.1, §8.4 private-price isolation | API-M08-37 |
| D-151 | For assisted orders / WhatsApp draft baskets, is stock **reserved when the secure checkout link is sent** or **when the customer confirms**? | MK:erp-support.html m-basket "reserved 30 min", link & reservation expiry options; BP §29.5 "customer opens a secure checkout link, confirms address and final price… same reservation, payment and fulfilment controls as website checkout"; BP §13.2 draft → confirm → reservation; durations D-026 | API-M10-20, API-M10-22, A08 |
| D-152 | **Export controls**: row limits, download-link expiry, which datasets require approval (full PII), watermarking of dealer price-list exports | BP §14.4 "row limits, secure download expiry, and audit history"; BP §18.1 "Export customer data"; MK samples (24 h links, ≤ 50,000 rows, "full PII export needs approval") are not requirements | API-M18-03, API-M18-05, API-M05-05 |
| D-153 | *(Complements D-130 in `03-database.md`, which covers storage protection at rest.)* **Sensitive-field masking & reveal policy**: which fields are masked for which roles (contact data, addresses, manufacturer serials, bank details), allowed reveal reasons, reveal duration | BP §18.2 "sensitive-field restrictions", §13.4, §19.1; MK:erp-customers.html (Reveal), erp-inventory.html (reveal manufacturer serial) — durations are samples | API-M02-31, masking in API-M08-28/29, API-M06-07, API-M14-33 |
| D-154 | **Quote validity window & re-quote policy**: how long a quote version is valid, alignment with reservation expiry, which changes count as "material" and need buyer acceptance | BP §8.1 step 7 "lock a time-limited quote version where supported", step 8 "notify the buyer of material changes"; BP §8.4 "Quantity changes after quote"; MK:store-checkout.html "Accept new total · quote Q-…-v2" | API-M05-01, API-M05-02, API-M10-06 (PRICE_CHANGED) |

## 7. Coverage summary

### 7.1 BP §17.4 documented contracts → endpoint IDs

|---|---|---|
| Search catalog | `GET /v1/products` | API-M21-01 |
| Product offer | `GET /v1/products/{id}/offers` | API-M04-03 |
| Quote basket | `POST /v1/quotes` | API-M05-01 |
| Place pending order | `POST /v1/orders` | API-M10-06 |
| Read order | `GET /v1/orders/{id}` | API-M10-07 |

| Payment webhook | `POST /v1/webhooks/payment-provider` | API-M11-03 |
| Shipping webhook | `POST /v1/webhooks/shipping-provider` | API-M12-16 |

## 8. Gap resolution (2026-09-27)

Resolution of the "API gaps found" sections of the second-wave plan files. Rule applied: a gap becomes an endpoint
(new ID, never renumbering existing IDs) or an extension of an existing endpoint only if a source supports it (BP,
MEET, PR, a mockup screen action, or a CONDITIONAL/MOCKUP-ONLY feature with its decision); otherwise it is rejected
with the reason. **65 endpoints added** (427 → 492).

### 8.2 Other changes made in this revision
- **Authorisation aligned with `07-auth-roles-permissions.md`:** every Auth·Authz cell is now generated from 07 §5
  (roles and cell codes per permission key) and §13 (auth class, key, scope). This applies BP §18.1 as 07 reads it —
  e.g. R-branch_manager may create product drafts (API-M04-16/-18/-20) and record receipts, and R-ops_admin/R-owner
  may record receipts (API-M07-09/-11/-12/-13) — and replaces the non-registry labels "buyer roles", "pricing roles",
  "catalog roles", "returns desk", "lead" with R-* roles plus designations (07 §4.3; D-222).
- **Permission keys proposed for the new endpoints (to register in 07 §5/§13):** `staff.assignable.read`,
  `catalog.tax.manage`, `purchasing.supplier.manage`, `dealer.order.approve`, `business.account.change_request`,
  `order.verification_flag`, `terms.read`, `terms.manage`, `fulfilment_task.manage`, `workspace.counts`,
  `report.definition.manage`, `template.document.read`, `template.document.manage`, `change.read`, `change.decide`,
  `system.alerts.manage`. The other new endpoints reuse existing 07 keys.
- **Decision aliases replaced:** D-140 → D-129, D-143 → D-121, D-144 → D-131, D-148 → D-137 (D-120, D-180, D-182 were
  not used here).
- **Vendor-model enum** everywhere: `supplier`, `supplier_fulfilment` (pilot), `marketplace_seller` (LATER).
- **Registry alignment:** "(req)" markers removed; accepted second-wave entities used (§5); build conditions aligned —
  API-M12-05/-14 MOCKUP-ONLY D-132, API-M08-30 and API-M10-16 MOCKUP-ONLY D-134, API-M08-14/-15/-43 MOCKUP-ONLY D-133,
  API-M14-25/-26 MOCKUP-ONLY D-131.
- **New pages mapped:** P-S14 (API-M02-02/-03/-08/-16/-17/-38), P-S15 (API-M04-02, API-M27-01), P-E16 and P-V05
  (API-M02-02…-09, -12…-17, -33, -38) — see §7.3.

## 9. SaaS API additions (2026-09-28, `D-227`)

Two API surfaces now exist. They share the conventions of §1 (`D-080`, `D-079`: base path and versioning,
pagination, error envelope with correlation id, money and date formats, idempotency transport, optimistic
concurrency) because one convention across two applications is cheaper than two — but they are different
applications on different hostnames with different identity realms (`19` §2 SEP-3).

### 9.1 Store API — what changes

| Change | Detail |
|---|---|
| Store scope | Every request is resolved to exactly one store by host before routing. No endpoint takes a store identifier as a parameter, and none accepts one from the client (BP §17.4: never trust a client-sent scope) |
| Capability gating | Every store-facing endpoint declares the capability it belongs to. When that capability is off, the route is not registered and the response is **404**, with no message naming the feature (`19` §9 INV-3). 403 is reserved for "the store has it, you may not" |
| Check order | store resolution → capability → authentication → permission → validation → business rule |
| Configuration exposure | A store-facing endpoint may return the store's **effective** configuration only. It never returns the catalogue of what is possible, a list of disabled features, another store's data, pack or template identifiers, or capability identifiers (`19` §9 INV-2) |
| Error messages | Rendered from terminology tokens; they never contain platform vocabulary (`19` §9 INV-5) |
| New endpoints | Store settings read/write for the L4 subset only (`T-1A.1-M31-05`), consumed by P-E15 |
| Rate limiting | Applied per store as well as per client, so one store cannot exhaust another's budget (`D-084`) |

### 9.2 Platform API — `API-M34-*`, `API-M35-*` (root admin only)

Catalogued in `20-root-admin.md` §8. It is never served on a store hostname, never reachable from a store
runtime, and authenticates only platform users (`D-245`) or the store-runtime callback credential used for
`config.applied`.

| Group | Range | Screens |
|---|---|---|
| Stores | API-M34-01…09 | P-R03, P-R05 |
| Configuration (draft, validate, compile, diff, publish, versions, rollback) | API-M34-10…19 | P-R05 |
| Vertical packs | API-M34-20…27 | P-R06 |
| Templates | API-M34-28…35 | P-R07 |
| Capabilities | API-M34-36…41 | P-R08, P-R05 |
| Terminology | API-M34-42…47 | P-R09, P-R05 |
| Branding | API-M34-48…53 | P-R05, P-R04 |
| Platform users, roles, audit | API-M34-54…60 | P-R11 |
| Support access | API-M34-61…64 | P-R11 |
| Deployment | API-M35-01…08 | P-R10 |
| Domains & TLS | API-M35-09…14 | P-R05 |
| Fleet (instance registry, `config.applied` intake, drift, retention) | API-M35-15…18 | P-R12 |

Each endpoint is contracted in full when its owning task is picked (protocol step 13), in the same format as the
store endpoints above. Mutating platform endpoints are audited (`BR-M34-01`); publish and deployment endpoints
are idempotent (`D-079`); two-person actions cannot be completed through a single authenticated call.

---

## 10. Feature-control, channel and automation API additions (2026-09-28, `D-273`, `D-274`, `D-278`)

### 10.1 Store API — new endpoint groups

| Group | Endpoints | Consumed by | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Store features | list delegated items with their current value; set a delegated item; read the open-work impact before switching one off | P-E15 Features (`T-1A.16-M24-09`) | Returns **only** delegated items. A non-delegated item behaves exactly as an unknown one (404) |
| Store channels | list the store's channels; create/update a binding; start and read sender verification; list template requirements and their state | P-E15 Channels (`T-1A.16-M24-11`) | Credentials are write-only; a read never returns a secret |
| Store templates | list, read, create version, submit for approval, read approval state | P-E15 Templates | Versioned; provider approval state reported honestly |
| Store automations | list granted automations; preview; enable/pause; read the run log | P-E14 | Only automations whose capability resolves on appear |
| Delivery status intake | provider webhooks per channel | Providers | Signed, replay-safe, store-scoped by the binding, idempotent on the provider message id |
| Store public API (`CAP-STORE_API`, `D-278`) | the client's own read/write access under capability-aware scopes | The client's systems | Per-store keys, per-store rate limits, full audit, same 404-not-403 rule |
| Outbound webhooks (`CAP-WEBHOOKS`, `D-278`) | subscription CRUD; delivery log; replay | The client's systems | Signed, retried with backoff, auto-suspended on repeated failure |

### 10.2 Rules that apply to all of them

| # | Rule |
|---|---|
| 1 | Every endpoint declares its capability. When the capability is off the route is not registered and the answer is **404** — including the delivery-status intake, so a provider cannot be used to probe which channels a store has |
| 2 | No endpoint returns a secret, a credential, another store's data, or the catalogue of what is possible — only the store's **effective** set (`19` §9 INV-2) |
| 3 | Send-related endpoints are idempotent on the message key; webhook intake is idempotent on the provider message id (`D-079`) |
| 4 | Rate limits apply per store as well as per client, and per channel (`19` §20) |
| 5 | Error messages are rendered from terminology tokens and name no platform concept |

### 10.3 Platform API — new group

`API-M34-74…80`: channel bindings per store, sender verification, template requirements, integration bindings,
automation grants, and the publish-check report that lists what a granted channel is still missing
(`20-root-admin.md` §8).

---

## 11. Client feedback CF1 API additions (2026-09-30, `D-282`–`D-286`)

Source: `docs/CLIENT_FEEDBACK_2026-09-30.md` (`CF1 §2` employee monitoring, `CF1 §3` analytics dashboard). Nine
endpoints added (492 → 501), never renumbering existing IDs. Each is catalogued in §2 and §3 in the usual format;
this table adds the gating, the permission key (registered in `07` §5.5 and §13) and the task that builds it.
Screens: `04c` §22 (P-E17) and §23 (P-E18); capabilities: `19` §5.3 O and Q; feature map: `21` §4.

| ID | Endpoint | Capability (off → 404) | Permission key | Built by | Blocking decisions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| API-M02-43 | `POST /v1/me/presence` | `CAP-STAFF_PRESENCE` (with `CAP-TEAM_MONITOR`) | `presence.self` | T-1A.2-M02-11 | D-283 |
| API-M09-03 | `POST /v1/storefront/visit-events` | `CAP-STOREFRONT_VISIT_ANALYTICS` (CANDIDATE) | `public.visit` | T-1A.9-M09-14 (CONDITIONAL) | D-285 (D-277 for a third-party tool) |
| API-M18-20 | `GET /v1/analytics/{view}` | `CAP-ANALYTICS_DASHBOARD`; view `customers` `CAP-CUSTOMER_ANALYTICS`; visit cards `CAP-STOREFRONT_VISIT_ANALYTICS` | `analytics.read`, `analytics.margin.read` | T-1A.15-M18-10 (data: T-1A.15-M18-09) | D-004, D-286 |
| API-M24-15 | `GET /v1/team/overview` | `CAP-TEAM_MONITOR` | `team.monitor.read` | T-1A.16-M24-12 | D-004, D-283 |
| API-M24-16 | `GET /v1/team/members/{user_id}/activity` | `CAP-TEAM_MONITOR` | `team.monitor.read` | T-1A.16-M24-12 | D-004, D-283 |
| API-M24-17 | `GET /v1/team/activity` | `CAP-TEAM_MONITOR` | `team.monitor.read` | T-1A.16-M24-12 | D-004, D-283 |
| API-M24-18 | `GET /v1/team/alert-rules` | `CAP-STAFF_ACTIVITY_ALERTS` | `team.monitor.read` | T-1A.14-M17-13 | D-283 |
| API-M24-19 | `PATCH /v1/team/alert-rules/{rule_key}` | `CAP-STAFF_ACTIVITY_ALERTS` | `team.alert.manage` | T-1A.14-M17-13 | D-283 |
| API-M24-20 | `POST /v1/team/alerts/{alert_id}/acknowledgement` | `CAP-STAFF_ACTIVITY_ALERTS` | `team.alert.ack` | T-1A.14-M17-13 | D-283 |

| # | Rule |
|---|---|
| 1 | **P-E17 and P-E18 follow the standard order** of `07` "SaaS additions" §B: capability off → 404 (screen, navigation entry and endpoints absent); capability on but the caller lacks `team.monitor.read` / `analytics.read` → **403** and no navigation entry; a person or location outside the caller's scope → 404 (object level). The existence of P-E17 is not hidden from staff — the staff notice (D-283 e) tells them what is recorded and who may see it |
| 2 | **Nothing outside the D-282 recorded list is accepted or returned.** API-M02-43 carries only a heartbeat, a self-set status and the current workspace area; no endpoint accepts or returns screen contents, keystrokes, camera or microphone data, device inventory, other apps or location beyond the sign-in network/branch label, or private messages |
| 3 | The activity feed and the person timeline are views of E-audit_event (area and work-item tags added by T-1A.2-M02-11); there is no second activity log |
| 4 | API-M18-20 reads only the rebuildable read models of T-1A.15-M18-09 (E-analytics_daily_fact, E-customer_cohort_snapshot) so P-E18 never scans transactional tables on request (D-268) |
| 5 | API-M09-03 exists only if D-285 chooses option (b) or (c); under (a) or (d) the endpoint and T-1A.9-M09-14 are `NOT_APPLICABLE` (D-213) and the P-E18 cards marked "Needs visit tracking" are absent |
| 6 | Exports from both screens are API-M18-03 jobs (datasets `team_activity`, `analytics.<view>`); saved views on P-E18 are API-M18-13/14 with screen_key `analytics`; "Schedule email" opens P-E13 `#schedules` (API-M18-07 with the saved view) |
| 7 | P-E17 actions that change other records use the existing endpoints: "Sign out everywhere" API-M02-23 (`admin.users.security_actions`), audit-log links API-M02-30, alert delivery settings API-M17-26 (owner digest) and API-M20-09 (the recipient's channels). "Reassign open items" and "Message" have no endpoint (`04c` §22.12) |
